# Dominant power: 0 contested, 1 Russia, 2 China, 3 Europe, 4 USA (must lead strictly and have at least 20)
SAC_africa_recalc = {
	set_variable = { global.AFR_DZ_dom = 0 }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.AFR_DZ_SOV > 19 } check_variable = { global.AFR_DZ_SOV > global.AFR_DZ_PRC } check_variable = { global.AFR_DZ_SOV > global.AFR_DZ_EUR } check_variable = { global.AFR_DZ_SOV > global.AFR_DZ_USA } } set_variable = { global.AFR_DZ_dom = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.AFR_DZ_PRC > 19 } check_variable = { global.AFR_DZ_PRC > global.AFR_DZ_SOV } check_variable = { global.AFR_DZ_PRC > global.AFR_DZ_EUR } check_variable = { global.AFR_DZ_PRC > global.AFR_DZ_USA } } set_variable = { global.AFR_DZ_dom = 2 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.AFR_DZ_EUR > 19 } check_variable = { global.AFR_DZ_EUR > global.AFR_DZ_SOV } check_variable = { global.AFR_DZ_EUR > global.AFR_DZ_PRC } check_variable = { global.AFR_DZ_EUR > global.AFR_DZ_USA } } set_variable = { global.AFR_DZ_dom = 3 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.AFR_DZ_USA > 19 } check_variable = { global.AFR_DZ_USA > global.AFR_DZ_SOV } check_variable = { global.AFR_DZ_USA > global.AFR_DZ_PRC } check_variable = { global.AFR_DZ_USA > global.AFR_DZ_EUR } } set_variable = { global.AFR_DZ_dom = 4 } }
	set_variable = { global.AFR_AO_dom = 0 }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.AFR_AO_SOV > 19 } check_variable = { global.AFR_AO_SOV > global.AFR_AO_PRC } check_variable = { global.AFR_AO_SOV > global.AFR_AO_EUR } check_variable = { global.AFR_AO_SOV > global.AFR_AO_USA } } set_variable = { global.AFR_AO_dom = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.AFR_AO_PRC > 19 } check_variable = { global.AFR_AO_PRC > global.AFR_AO_SOV } check_variable = { global.AFR_AO_PRC > global.AFR_AO_EUR } check_variable = { global.AFR_AO_PRC > global.AFR_AO_USA } } set_variable = { global.AFR_AO_dom = 2 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.AFR_AO_EUR > 19 } check_variable = { global.AFR_AO_EUR > global.AFR_AO_SOV } check_variable = { global.AFR_AO_EUR > global.AFR_AO_PRC } check_variable = { global.AFR_AO_EUR > global.AFR_AO_USA } } set_variable = { global.AFR_AO_dom = 3 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.AFR_AO_USA > 19 } check_variable = { global.AFR_AO_USA > global.AFR_AO_SOV } check_variable = { global.AFR_AO_USA > global.AFR_AO_PRC } check_variable = { global.AFR_AO_USA > global.AFR_AO_EUR } } set_variable = { global.AFR_AO_dom = 4 } }
	set_variable = { global.AFR_BJ_dom = 0 }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.AFR_BJ_SOV > 19 } check_variable = { global.AFR_BJ_SOV > global.AFR_BJ_PRC } check_variable = { global.AFR_BJ_SOV > global.AFR_BJ_EUR } check_variable = { global.AFR_BJ_SOV > global.AFR_BJ_USA } } set_variable = { global.AFR_BJ_dom = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.AFR_BJ_PRC > 19 } check_variable = { global.AFR_BJ_PRC > global.AFR_BJ_SOV } check_variable = { global.AFR_BJ_PRC > global.AFR_BJ_EUR } check_variable = { global.AFR_BJ_PRC > global.AFR_BJ_USA } } set_variable = { global.AFR_BJ_dom = 2 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.AFR_BJ_EUR > 19 } check_variable = { global.AFR_BJ_EUR > global.AFR_BJ_SOV } check_variable = { global.AFR_BJ_EUR > global.AFR_BJ_PRC } check_variable = { global.AFR_BJ_EUR > global.AFR_BJ_USA } } set_variable = { global.AFR_BJ_dom = 3 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.AFR_BJ_USA > 19 } check_variable = { global.AFR_BJ_USA > global.AFR_BJ_SOV } check_variable = { global.AFR_BJ_USA > global.AFR_BJ_PRC } check_variable = { global.AFR_BJ_USA > global.AFR_BJ_EUR } } set_variable = { global.AFR_BJ_dom = 4 } }
	set_variable = { global.AFR_BW_dom = 0 }

# The USA no longer exists: its influence is split equally between the remaining powers
SAC_africa_usa_split = {
	set_temp_variable = { afr_n = 0 }
	if = { limit = { OR = { country_exists = SOV country_exists = WAG } } add_to_temp_variable = { afr_n = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = PRC } add_to_temp_variable = { afr_n = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = FRA } add_to_temp_variable = { afr_n = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { afr_n > 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { afr_share = global.AFR_AO_USA }
		divide_temp_variable = { afr_share = afr_n }
		if = { limit = { OR = { country_exists = SOV country_exists = WAG } } add_to_variable = { global.AFR_AO_SOV = afr_share } clamp_variable = { var = global.AFR_AO_SOV min = 0 max = 100 } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = PRC } add_to_variable = { global.AFR_AO_PRC = afr_share } clamp_variable = { var = global.AFR_AO_PRC min = 0 max = 100 } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = FRA } add_to_variable = { global.AFR_AO_EUR = afr_share } clamp_variable = { var = global.AFR_AO_EUR min = 0 max = 100 } }

		if = { limit = { country_exists = FRA } add_to_variable = { global.AFR_SZ_EUR = afr_share } clamp_variable = { var = global.AFR_SZ_EUR min = 0 max = 100 } }
		set_variable = { global.AFR_SZ_USA = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { afr_share = global.AFR_TD_USA }
		divide_temp_variable = { afr_share = afr_n }
		if = { limit = { OR = { country_exists = SOV country_exists = WAG } } add_to_variable = { global.AFR_TD_SOV = afr_share } clamp_variable = { var = global.AFR_TD_SOV min = 0 max = 100 } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = PRC } add_to_variable = { global.AFR_TD_PRC = afr_share } clamp_variable = { var = global.AFR_TD_PRC min = 0 max = 100 } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = FRA } add_to_variable = { global.AFR_TD_EUR = afr_share } clamp_variable = { var = global.AFR_TD_EUR min = 0 max = 100 } }
		set_variable = { global.AFR_TD_USA = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { afr_share = global.AFR_TG_USA }
		divide_temp_variable = { afr_share = afr_n }
		if = { limit = { OR = { country_exists = SOV country_exists = WAG } } add_to_variable = { global.AFR_TG_SOV = afr_share } clamp_variable = { var = global.AFR_TG_SOV min = 0 max = 100 } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = PRC } add_to_variable = { global.AFR_TG_PRC = afr_share } clamp_variable = { var = global.AFR_TG_PRC min = 0 max = 100 } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = FRA } add_to_variable = { global.AFR_TG_EUR = afr_share } clamp_variable = { var = global.AFR_TG_EUR min = 0 max = 100 } }
		set_variable = { global.AFR_TG_USA = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { afr_share = global.AFR_TN_USA }
		divide_temp_variable = { afr_share = afr_n }
		if = { limit = { OR = { country_exists = SOV country_exists = WAG } } add_to_variable = { global.AFR_TN_SOV = afr_share } clamp_variable = { var = global.AFR_TN_SOV min = 0 max = 100 } }

		if = { limit = { country_exists = FRA } add_to_variable = { global.AFR_TN_EUR = afr_share } clamp_variable = { var = global.AFR_TN_EUR min = 0 max = 100 } }
		set_variable = { global.AFR_TN_USA = 0 }

		divide_temp_variable = { afr_share = afr_n }

		if = { limit = { country_exists = PRC } add_to_variable = { global.AFR_TZ_PRC = afr_share } clamp_variable = { var = global.AFR_TZ_PRC min = 0 max = 100 } }

		if = { limit = { country_exists = PRC } add_to_variable = { global.AFR_UG_PRC = afr_share } clamp_variable = { var = global.AFR_UG_PRC min = 0 max = 100 } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = FRA } add_to_variable = { global.AFR_UG_EUR = afr_share } clamp_variable = { var = global.AFR_UG_EUR min = 0 max = 100 } }

		if = { limit = { country_exists = FRA } add_to_variable = { global.AFR_ZA_EUR = afr_share } clamp_variable = { var = global.AFR_ZA_EUR min = 0 max = 100 } }

		if = { limit = { country_exists = PRC } add_to_variable = { global.AFR_ZM_PRC = afr_share } clamp_variable = { var = global.AFR_ZM_PRC min = 0 max = 100 } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = FRA } add_to_variable = { global.AFR_ZM_EUR = afr_share } clamp_variable = { var = global.AFR_ZM_EUR min = 0 max = 100 } }

		set_temp_variable = { afr_share = global.AFR_ZW_USA }
		divide_temp_variable = { afr_share = afr_n }

		if = { limit = { country_exists = PRC } add_to_variable = { global.AFR_ZW_PRC = afr_share } clamp_variable = { var = global.AFR_ZW_PRC min = 0 max = 100 } }
		if = { limit = { country_exists = FRA } add_to_variable = { global.AFR_ZW_EUR = afr_share } clamp_variable = { var = global.AFR_ZW_EUR min = 0 max = 100 } }
		set_variable = { global.AFR_ZW_USA = 0 }
	}
	SAC_africa_recalc = yes
}

SAC_africa_gain_AO = {
	if = { limit = { OR = { tag = WAG AND = { tag = SOV NOT = { has_global_flag = WAG_mutiny_failed } } } } add_to_variable = { var = global.AFR_AO_SOV value = afr_gain } clamp_variable = { var = global.AFR_AO_SOV min = 0 max = 100 } }

	SAC_africa_recalc = yes
	SAC_africa_thresholds_AO = yes
}

	if = { limit = { OR = { tag = PRC } } add_to_variable = { var = global.AFR_SZ_PRC value = afr_gain } clamp_variable = { var = global.AFR_SZ_PRC min = 0 max = 100 } }
	if = { limit = { OR = { tag = FRA } } add_to_variable = { var = global.AFR_SZ_EUR value = afr_gain } clamp_variable = { var = global.AFR_SZ_EUR min = 0 max = 100 } }
	if = { limit = { OR = { tag = USA } } add_to_variable = { var = global.AFR_SZ_USA value = afr_gain } clamp_variable = { var = global.AFR_SZ_USA min = 0 max = 100 } }
	SAC_africa_recalc = yes
	SAC_africa_thresholds_SZ = yes
}

# Influence levels for the acting country (ROOT): 60+ military access, 80+ the country becomes a puppet
SAC_africa_thresholds_AO = {
	if = { limit = { country_exists = ANG NOT = { tag = ANG } NOT = { has_war_with = ANG } SAC_afr_inf_AO_60 = yes } ANG = { give_military_access = ROOT } }
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = ANG NOT = { tag = ANG } NOT = { has_war_with = ANG } SAC_afr_inf_AO_80 = yes ANG = { is_subject = no is_major = no has_war = no } }
		if = { limit = { tag = WAG country_exists = SOV is_subject_of = SOV } SOV = { puppet = ANG } }
		else = { puppet = ANG }
		custom_effect_tooltip = SAC_africa_puppet_tt
	}
}

	if = { limit = { country_exists = VOL NOT = { tag = VOL } NOT = { has_war_with = VOL } SAC_afr_inf_BF_60 = yes } VOL = { give_military_access = ROOT } }

		if = { limit = { tag = WAG country_exists = SOV is_subject_of = SOV } SOV = { puppet = KEN } }

# Monthly: trade deals end when influence drops below 30 or the country is gone (resources are taken back)
SAC_africa_monthly_deals = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { AFR_deal_AO > 0 } NOT = { AND = { country_exists = ANG SAC_afr_inf_AO_30 = yes } } }
		var:AFR_deal_state_AO = { add_resource = { type = oil amount = -38 } }
		set_variable = { AFR_deal_AO = 0 }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { AFR_deal_BW > 0 } NOT = { AND = { country_exists = BOT SAC_afr_inf_BW_30 = yes } } }
		var:AFR_deal_state_BW = { add_resource = { type = chromium amount = -1 } add_resource = { type = steel amount = -1 } }
		set_variable = { AFR_deal_BW = 0 }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { AFR_deal_CD > 0 } NOT = { AND = { country_exists = COG SAC_afr_inf_CD_30 = yes } } }
		var:AFR_deal_state_CD = { add_resource = { type = tungsten amount = -18 } add_resource = { type = oil amount = -1 } add_resource = { type = steel amount = -1 } }
		set_variable = { AFR_deal_CD = 0 }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { AFR_deal_CG > 0 } NOT = { AND = { country_exists = RCG SAC_afr_inf_CG_30 = yes } } }
		var:AFR_deal_state_CG = { add_resource = { type = oil amount = -8 } }
		set_variable = { AFR_deal_CG = 0 }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { AFR_deal_CI > 0 } NOT = { AND = { country_exists = IVO SAC_afr_inf_CI_30 = yes } } }
		var:AFR_deal_state_CI = { add_resource = { type = rubber amount = -4 } add_resource = { type = oil amount = -1 } }
		set_variable = { AFR_deal_CI = 0 }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { AFR_deal_CM > 0 } NOT = { AND = { country_exists = CMR SAC_afr_inf_CM_30 = yes } } }
		var:AFR_deal_state_CM = { add_resource = { type = oil amount = -2 } add_resource = { type = aluminium amount = -1 } }
		set_variable = { AFR_deal_CM = 0 }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { AFR_deal_DZ > 0 } NOT = { AND = { country_exists = ALG SAC_afr_inf_DZ_30 = yes } } }
		var:AFR_deal_state_DZ = { add_resource = { type = oil amount = -29 } }
		set_variable = { AFR_deal_DZ = 0 }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { AFR_deal_EG > 0 } NOT = { AND = { country_exists = EGY SAC_afr_inf_EG_30 = yes } } }
		var:AFR_deal_state_EG = { add_resource = { type = steel amount = -6 } add_resource = { type = oil amount = -12 } }
		set_variable = { AFR_deal_EG = 0 }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { AFR_deal_ET > 0 } NOT = { AND = { country_exists = ETH SAC_afr_inf_ET_30 = yes } } }
		var:AFR_deal_state_ET = { add_resource = { type = steel amount = -3 } add_resource = { type = aluminium amount = -1 } }
		set_variable = { AFR_deal_ET = 0 }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { AFR_deal_GA > 0 } NOT = { AND = { country_exists = GAB SAC_afr_inf_GA_30 = yes } } }
		var:AFR_deal_state_GA = { add_resource = { type = oil amount = -5 } }
		set_variable = { AFR_deal_GA = 0 }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { AFR_deal_GH > 0 } NOT = { AND = { country_exists = GHA SAC_afr_inf_GH_30 = yes } } }
		var:AFR_deal_state_GH = { add_resource = { type = aluminium amount = -2 } add_resource = { type = rubber amount = -2 } add_resource = { type = oil amount = -2 } }
		set_variable = { AFR_deal_GH = 0 }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { AFR_deal_GN > 0 } NOT = { AND = { country_exists = GNA SAC_afr_inf_GN_30 = yes } } }
		var:AFR_deal_state_GN = { add_resource = { type = rubber amount = -2 } }
		set_variable = { AFR_deal_GN = 0 }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { AFR_deal_GQ > 0 } NOT = { AND = { country_exists = EQG SAC_afr_inf_GQ_30 = yes } } }
		var:AFR_deal_state_GQ = { add_resource = { type = oil amount = -6 } }
		set_variable = { AFR_deal_GQ = 0 }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { AFR_deal_KE > 0 } NOT = { AND = { country_exists = KEN SAC_afr_inf_KE_30 = yes } } }
		var:AFR_deal_state_KE = { add_resource = { type = steel amount = -1 } }
		set_variable = { AFR_deal_KE = 0 }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { AFR_deal_LR > 0 } NOT = { AND = { country_exists = LIB SAC_afr_inf_LR_30 = yes } } }
		var:AFR_deal_state_LR = { add_resource = { type = rubber amount = -3 } }
		set_variable = { AFR_deal_LR = 0 }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { AFR_deal_LY > 0 } NOT = { AND = { country_exists = LBA SAC_afr_inf_LY_30 = yes } } }

	TUN = { capital_scope = { if = { limit = { NOT = { free_building_slots = { building = power_plant size > 0 include_locked = no } } } add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1 } add_building_construction = { type = power_plant level = 1 instant_build = yes } } }
	set_variable = { AFR_cd_power_TN = 30 }
	SAC_africa_start_ticker = yes
	set_temp_variable = { afr_gain = 5 }
	SAC_africa_gain_TN = yes
	TUN = { add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = SAC_africa_aid_opinion } }
}

	GNB = { add_manpower = -3000 } add_manpower = 3000
	set_variable = { AFR_cd_recruit_GW = 30 }
	SAC_africa_start_ticker = yes
	set_temp_variable = { afr_gain = 0 }
	SAC_africa_gain_GW = yes
	GNB = { add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = SAC_africa_aid_opinion } }
}

# Civil war sides: the government plus every enemy fighting it (neighbours, African enemies, or occupiers). Stored on ROOT.
SAC_africa_build_sides_AO = {
	clear_array = AFR_sides
	clear_variable = AFR_gov
	if = { limit = { country_exists = ANG }
		set_variable = { AFR_gov = ANG }
		add_to_array = { AFR_sides = ANG }
		ANG = { every_enemy_country = { limit = { OR = { is_neighbor_of = ANG capital_scope = { is_on_continent = africa } any_controlled_state = { is_owned_by = ANG } } } add_to_array = { ROOT.AFR_sides = THIS } } }
	}
}

# Starts the daily cooldown countdown for ROOT (hidden event sac_afr.1 re-fires each day while a cooldown runs)
SAC_africa_start_ticker = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = SAC_afr_ticking } } set_country_flag = SAC_afr_ticking country_event = { id = sac_afr.1 days = 1 } }
}
